> `#AI 大模型` `#Benchmark` `#自动化工具` 
> Mengdeng-145 is an AI model IQ benchmark tool designed to measure cognitive hard metrics, including cognitive reflection, worst-case guarantee, anti-memorization, spatial drawing, and instruction following. It uses 18 dynamically parameterized units with solver-generated answers, avoiding static test leakage. Users can run parallel evaluations locally by simply providing their API base URL and key, with all scoring and timing performed in the browser. The tool also includes a channel identification module that probes model knowledge cutoff, potential internet access, Codex reverse-proxy signatures, and IQ degradation. Built on TanStack Start, it provides detailed HTML reports and a public leaderboard, making it suitable for model comparison and channel quality auditing.

> Ponytail is a skill for AI coding agents that instills the mindset of a lazy senior developer, ensuring agents write the least amount of code necessary and reuse existing solutions instead of over-engineering. Built as a prompt engineering technique, it works with Claude Code and 20+ other agents, preserving all safety guards. In realistic benchmarks on a FastAPI+React repo, it cut lines of code by 54% (up to 94% in over-building cases), token usage by 22%, cost by 20%, and time by 27% compared to a no-skill baseline.
> <sub>Makes your AI agent think like the laziest senior dev in the room. The best code is the code you never wrote.</sub>

> routatic-proxy is a Go CLI proxy that sits between Claude Code and upstream providers such as OpenCode Go, OpenCode Zen, AWS Bedrock, OpenRouter, and Anthropic. It transparently intercepts Anthropic-format requests, converts them to provider-native formats, and routes automatically based on context, including scenario-specific routing for default, thinking, long context, and background tasks. Key features include streaming, tool calling, fallback chains, circuit breaker, and hot reload. This enables developers to use Claude Code's familiar interface while leveraging diverse models, cost-effective pricing, and enterprise infrastructure.

The output includes all traces, the deliberation transcript, and final Markdown, HTML, and PDF reports. Deliberation is generative, regularly producing answers not found in any individual trace. Ideal for technical selection, architecture decisions, and analytical validation, it dramatically improves answer depth and reliability compared to single-shot inference.

> Claude Context is an MCP-based semantic code search tool designed for Claude Code and other AI coding agents. It indexes the entire codebase into a vector database using embeddings, then retrieves only the most relevant code snippets as context for queries. This approach avoids loading entire directories, significantly reducing API costs and latency for large projects while improving code understanding and generation accuracy. It seamlessly integrates with Claude Code via the Model Context Protocol and supports Zilliz Cloud Milvus as the vector store, making it an essential tool for developers working with massive codebases.
> <sub>Code search MCP for Claude Code. Make entire codebase the context for any coding agent.</sub>

> Ralph is an autonomous AI agent loop that repeatedly invokes AI coding tools like Amp or Claude Code until all PRD items are completed. Each iteration runs with a fresh, clean context, while memory and knowledge persist via git history, progress.txt, and prd.json. The loop selects the highest-priority unfinished user story, implements it, runs quality checks (typecheck and tests), commits when successful, and updates the task list. This enables automated, self-driving development workflows that can handle large stories beyond a single context window, making it ideal for AI-assisted coding and iterative software delivery.
> <sub>Ralph is an autonomous AI agent loop that runs repeatedly until all PRD items are complete.</sub>

> FireNio is an easy-to-use asynchronous I/O framework based on Java NIO and Linux epoll, designed to simplify the rapid development of network communication services. It offers a clean and concise API that enables developers to quickly build TCP, HTTP, and WebSocket servers with minimal effort. The framework comes with built-in automatic reconnection, heartbeat mechanisms, and SSL encryption (supporting both JdkSSL and OpenSSL). Additionally, it supports custom protocol extensions, including LengthValue and Protobase, for flexible data transmission. FireNio has demonstrated outstanding performance in TechEmpower Framework Benchmarks (TFB), making it an ideal choice for high-concurrency, low-latency server-side applications.
> <sub>🐳🐳An easy of use io framework project based on java nio&epoll</sub>

> This system is an online document preview and editing platform built with LibreOffice and CKEditor. It converts Office documents such as DOC/DOCX, XLS, PPT into HTML for in-browser editing, and supports versioning with save as new version or discard changes. A built-in watermark processor handles both text and image watermarks across images, Word, Excel, PPT, and PDF, enabling copyright protection. Additionally, PDF files can be converted to HTML for online editing. This solution is ideal for enterprise knowledge bases, OA systems, and any scenario requiring secure online document collaboration.
> <sub>文档在线预览编辑，文档水印 / Office files preview or edit online, doc/docx convert to html, watermark</sub>
